UNIT function 67
67: static void fk_die ( const char * msg ) {
68: long long n = 0 ;
69: while ( msg [ n ] ) {
70: n = n + 1 ;
71: }
72: write ( 2 , msg , n ) ;
73: write ( 2 , "\n" , 1 ) ;
74: exit ( 1 ) ;
75: }

UNIT function 246
246: static long long fk_host_cpu_busy_us ( void ) {
247: #ifdef __APPLE__
 int ticks [ 4 ] = { 0 , 0 , 0 , 0 } ;
249: unsigned int count = 4 ;
250: if ( host_statistics ( mach_host_self ( ) , 3 , ticks , & count ) ! = 0 ) { return - 1 ; }
251: return ( ( long long ) ( unsigned int ) ticks [ 0 ] + ( long long ) ( unsigned int ) ticks [ 1 ] + ( long long ) ( unsigned int ) ticks [ 3 ] ) * 10000 ;
252: #else
 return - 1 ;
254: #endif
 }

UNIT function 256
256: static long long fk_gpu_step ( long long now_us ) {
257: if ( fk_gpu_busy_last_us > 0 & & now_us - fk_gpu_busy_last_us < 50000 ) { return fk_gpu_level_cache ; }
258: long long level = fk_host_gpu_utilization ( ) ;
259: if ( fk_gpu_busy_last_us > 0 & & level > = 0 & & now_us > fk_gpu_busy_last_us ) { fk_gpu_busy_total_us = fk_gpu_busy_total_us + ( now_us - fk_gpu_busy_last_us ) * level / 100 ; }
260: fk_gpu_busy_last_us = now_us ;
261: fk_gpu_level_cache = level ;
262: return level ;
263: }

UNIT directive 314
314: #define FK_PATH_CAP 4096               /* one host path, or a path-shaped name (a .fkb/.sym/.dylib sibling, a temp spelling). Not a table: a path longer than this names nothing the host can open. Every fk_cstr into a path buffer refuses loudly past it and every join dies loudly rather than truncate -- a cut path names the wrong file. */

UNIT directive 315
315: #define FK_MESH_MSG_CAP 4096           /* one mesh/sense JSON line, built by sprintf from bounded fields (an ssid under 64 bytes and integers) */

UNIT directive 316
316: #define FK_MEM_CELL_CAP_INIT 4096      /* fk_mem: mutable record-cell table (tags 13/14). Birth size only -- the table grows to hold any written cell id (fk_mem_reserve). The old fixed table MASKED the id (mi & 4095): two cells 4096 apart silently ALIASED, one state quietly swapped for another. */

UNIT directive 317
317: #define FK_STAGED_INPUT_CAP_INIT 262144 /* fk_src: staged auxiliary input (the input_byte primitive); birth size only -- grows to carry any staged text (the old fixed buffer truncated it SILENTLY at the brim). */

UNIT directive 318
318: #define FK_VALUE_STACK_CAP_INIT 1048576  /* fk_vs: the evaluator's argument/value stack. Birth size only -- the stack DOUBLES on demand (fk_vs_grow, kernel_stat 27/28 = live cap / doublings), so stack slots are not a wall; runaway recursion is spoken for by the walker's own host-stack diagnostic. History: 65536->1048576 (2026-07-30) when the fourth kernel alone refused a 100,000-deep count the other three answered; a loud wall after that; growth since 2026-09-02. New slots are zeroed so a melt walking raised-but-unwritten slots sees the same inert 0 the BSS era gave it. */

UNIT directive 319
319: #define FK_STRING_POOL_INIT_BYTES 1048576 /* fk_sb: interned-string byte pool, initial size */

UNIT directive 320
320: #define FK_STRING_TABLE_INIT_CAP 16384  /* fk_so/fk_sl: interned-string table, initial entry count */

UNIT directive 328
328: #define FK_NODE_CAP_INIT 262144         /* fk_nkind, ncat, nkids, nval, nid, nsfile, nsline, nscol, nsattr, fbroots, nhash_memo, inram slot/generation/released: birth capacity only -- the table DOUBLES on demand (fk_nodes_grow), so there is no node wall. Handles are indices into column arrays; doubling the columns keeps every handle valid, and the intern index grows with them (held at 4x the node cap, rebuilt from fk_nhash_memo). History: 65536->262144 (2026-07-02) when a full table made every guard silently return handle 0; then a loud die at the wall; growth since 2026-09-02. A runaway consumer now shows as monotone kernel_stat 19/20 (live cap / doublings) instead of a refusal -- probe whether the fill POSITION moves, same discipline as ever. 262144*104B ~= 27MB at birth. */

UNIT function 427

428: return ( ( fk_fnbase - v ) - 1 ) > > 1 ;
429: }

UNIT function 430
430: static long long fk_is_fnval ( long long v ) {
431: if ( v > = fk_fnbase ) {
432: return 0 ;
433: }
434: if ( v < = fk_fnval_floor ) {
435: return 0 ;
436: }
437: if ( ( ( fk_fnbase - v ) & 1 ) = = 0 ) {
438: return 0 ;
439: }
440: long long fi = fk_fnval_idx ( v ) ;
441: if ( fi > = 0 & & fi < fk_fn_count ) {
442: return 1 ;
443: }
444: if ( fi > = FK_CLOSURE_IDX_BASE & & fi < FK_CLOSURE_IDX_BASE + fk_clo_top ) {
445: return 1 ;
446: }
447: return 0 ;
448: }

UNIT function 452
452: static long long fk_fnval_target ( long long v ) {
453: long long fi = fk_fnval_idx ( v ) ;
454: if ( fi < FK_CLOSURE_IDX_BASE ) {
455: return fi ;
456: }
457: return fk_clo_target [ fi - FK_CLOSURE_IDX_BASE ] ;
458: }

UNIT function 459
459: static long long fk_fnval_is_closure ( long long v ) {
460: return fk_fnval_idx ( v ) > = FK_CLOSURE_IDX_BASE ? 1 : 0 ;
461: }

UNIT declaration 470
470: static long long fk_call_cap_vals [ FK_CLOSURE_CAP_MAX ] ;

UNIT function 471
471: static void fk_clo_reserve ( long long needed ) {
472: if ( needed < = fk_clo_cap ) {
473: return ;
474: }
475: long long nc = fk_clo_cap = = 0 ? 64 : fk_clo_cap * 2 ;
476: while ( nc < needed ) {
477: nc = nc * 2 ;
478: }
479: fk_clo_target = ( long long * ) realloc ( fk_clo_target , ( unsigned long ) ( nc * 8 ) ) ;
480: fk_clo_capbase = ( long long * ) realloc ( fk_clo_capbase , ( unsigned long ) ( nc * 8 ) ) ;
481: fk_clo_capcount = ( long long * ) realloc ( fk_clo_capcount , ( unsigned long ) ( nc * 8 ) ) ;
482: if ( fk_clo_target = = 0 | | fk_clo_capbase = = 0 | | fk_clo_capcount = = 0 ) {
483: fk_die ( "fk_clo_reserve: out of memory growing the closure-instance table" ) ;
484: }
485: fk_clo_cap = nc ;
486: }

UNIT function 487
487: static long long fk_clo_capvals_reserve ( long long needed ) {
488: if ( needed < = fk_clo_capvals_cap ) {

621: }

UNIT function 656
656: static void fk_fmt_float_js ( double f , char * out ) {
657: if ( f ! = f ) {
658: out [ 0 ] = 'N' ; out [ 1 ] = 'a' ; out [ 2 ] = 'N' ; out [ 3 ] = 0 ;
659: return ;
660: }
661: if ( f > 1 . 7976931348623157e308 ) {
662: sprintf ( out , "Infinity" ) ;
663: return ;
664: }
665: if ( f < - 1 . 7976931348623157e308 ) {
666: sprintf ( out , "-Infinity" ) ;
667: return ;
668: }
669: char ebuf [ 64 ] ;
670: long long p = 0 ;
671: while ( p < = 17 ) {
672: sprintf ( ebuf , "%.*e" , ( int ) p , f ) ;
673: if ( strtod ( ebuf , ( char * * ) 0 ) = = f ) {
674: break ;
675: }

677: }
678: if ( p > 17 ) {
679: p = 17 ;
680: }

683: long long k = 0 ;
684: while ( ebuf [ k ] ! = 0 & & ebuf [ k ] ! = FK_CH_LOWER_E ) {
685: k = k + 1 ;
686: }

688: long long esign = 1 ;
689: if ( ebuf [ k ] = = FK_CH_LOWER_E ) {
690: k = k + 1 ;

693: k = k + 1 ;
694: } else if ( ebuf [ k ] = = FK_CH_PLUS ) {
695: k = k + 1 ;

698: exp10 = exp10 * 10 + ( ebuf [ k ] - FK_CH_DIGIT0 ) ;
699: k = k + 1 ;
700: }
701: exp10 = exp10 * esign ;
702: }

705: return ;
706: }
707: long long dp = exp10 + 1 ;

709: if ( prec < 0 ) {
710: prec = 0 ;
711: }
712: sprintf ( out , "%.*f" , ( int ) prec , f ) ;
713: }

1585: fk_die ( "fk_record_keys_reserve: out of memory growing a record's key row" ) ;
1586: }
1587: fk_rkcap [ r ] = nc ;
1588: }

UNIT function 1627
1627: static long long fk_bp_ideq ( long long a , long long b ) {
1628: if ( a < 0 & & b < 0 ) {
1629: long long ia = fk_nidx ( a ) ;
1630: long long ib = fk_nidx ( b ) ;
1631: if ( ia > = 1 & & ia < = fk_np & & ib > = 1 & & ib < = fk_np & &
1632: fk_nkind [ ia ] = = 3 & & fk_nkind [ ib ] = = 3 ) {
1633: return ( fk_nid [ ia ] [ 0 ] = = fk_nid [ ib ] [ 0 ] & & fk_nid [ ia ] [ 1 ] = = fk_nid [ ib ] [ 1 ] & &
1634: fk_nid [ ia ] [ 2 ] = = fk_nid [ ib ] [ 2 ] & & fk_nid [ ia ] [ 3 ] = = fk_nid [ ib ] [ 3 ] ) ? 1 : 0 ;
1635: }
1636: }
1637: return a = = b ? 1 : 0 ;
1638: }

UNIT function 1639

1643: return m ;
1644: }
1645: m = m + 1 ;
1646: }
1647: return 0 - 1 ;
1648: }

1652: n = n + 1 ;

UNIT function 1656
1656: static void fk_cstr ( long long sv , char * out , long long cap ) {
1657: long long sa = fk_stri ( sv ) ;
1658: long long n = 0 ;
1659: if ( sa > = 0 & & FK_SOK ( sa ) ) {
1660: n = FK_SLEN ( sa ) ;
1661: if ( n > cap - 1 ) {
1662: fk_die ( "fk_cstr: string longer than the destination buffer -- silently truncating would corrupt the path / hostname / port / URL / device-name the caller is about to use (every fk_cstr caller is one of these). Raise the caller's buffer if this length is legitimate." ) ;
1663: }
1664: long long j = 0 ;
1665: while ( j < n ) {
1666: out [ j ] = FK_SBYTES ( sa ) [ j ] ;
1667: j = j + 1 ;
1668: }
1669: }
1670: out [ n ] = 0 ;
1671: }

UNIT function 1677
1677: static char * fk_cstr_heap ( long long sv ) {
1678: long long sa = fk_stri ( sv ) ;
1679: long long n = ( sa > = 0 & & FK_SOK ( sa ) ) ? FK_SLEN ( sa ) : 0 ;
1680: char * out = malloc ( ( unsigned long ) ( n + 1 ) ) ;
1681: if ( out = = 0 ) {
1682: fk_die ( "fk_cstr_heap: out of memory" ) ;
1683: }
1684: long long j = 0 ;
1685: while ( j < n ) {
1686: out [ j ] = FK_SBYTES ( sa ) [ j ] ;
1687: j = j + 1 ;
1688: }
1689: out [ n ] = 0 ;
1690: return out ;
1691: }

1700: fk_die ( "fk_sb: out of memory growing string pool" ) ;

UNIT function 1703
1703: static long long fk_sbuf ( const char * buf , long long n ) {
1704: if ( n < 0 ) {
1705: n = 0 ;
1706: }
1707: fk_sinit ( ) ;
1708: while ( fk_sbp + n > fk_scap_b ) {
1709: fk_sb = ( char * ) fk_store_grow ( 's' , fk_sb , fk_scap_b , fk_scap_b * 2 , FK_STORE_STR_BYTES , 0 ) ;
1710: fk_scap_b = fk_scap_b * 2 ;
1711: fk_sb_check ( ) ;
1712: }
1713: long long j = 0 ;
1714: while ( j < n ) {
1715: fk_sb [ fk_sbp + j ] = buf [ j ] ;
1716: j = j + 1 ;
1717: }
1718: return fk_strv ( fk_sintern ( fk_sbp , n ) ) ;
1719: }

UNIT function 1826
1826: static int fk_metal_dyn_default_path ( char * out ) {
1827: const char * suffix = "form/native/metal/fk-metal-carrier.dylib" ;
1828: long long last_slash = - 1 ;
1829: long long i = 0 ;
1830: long long pos = 0 ;
1831: while ( fk_self_path [ i ] ! = 0 ) {
1832: if ( fk_self_path [ i ] = = '/' ) {
1833: last_slash = i ;
1834: }
1835: i = i + 1 ;
1836: }
1837: if ( last_slash > = 0 ) {
1838: i = 0 ;
1839: while ( i < = last_slash ) {
1840: if ( pos + 1 > = FK_PATH_CAP ) {
1841: fk_metal_dyn_reason = "dynamic carrier path exceeds FK_PATH_CAP" ;
1842: out [ 0 ] = 0 ;
1843: return 0 ;
1844: }
1845: out [ pos ] = fk_self_path [ i ] ;
1846: pos = pos + 1 ;
1847: i = i + 1 ;
1848: }
1849: }
1850: i = 0 ;
1851: while ( suffix [ i ] ! = 0 ) {
1852: if ( pos + 1 > = FK_PATH_CAP ) {
1853: fk_metal_dyn_reason = "dynamic carrier path exceeds FK_PATH_CAP" ;
1854: out [ 0 ] = 0 ;
1855: return 0 ;
1856: }
1857: out [ pos ] = suffix [ i ] ;
1858: pos = pos + 1 ;
1859: i = i + 1 ;
1860: }
1861: out [ pos ] = 0 ;
1862: return 1 ;
1863: }

UNIT function 1864
1864: static int fk_metal_dyn_load ( void ) {
1865: char * env ;
1866: char path [ FK_PATH_CAP ] ;
1867: if ( fk_metal_dyn_ready ) {
1868: return 1 ;
1869: }
1870: if ( fk_metal_dyn_attempted ) {
1871: return 0 ;
1872: }
1873: fk_metal_dyn_attempted = 1 ;
1874: env = getenv ( "FKWU_METAL_CARRIER" ) ;
1875: if ( env ! = 0 & & env [ 0 ] ! = 0 ) {
1876: return fk_metal_dyn_try_path ( env ) ;
1877: }
1878: if ( ! fk_metal_dyn_default_path ( path ) ) {
1879: return 0 ;
1880: }
1881: return fk_metal_dyn_try_path ( path ) ;
1882: }

3564: }

3566: ms = 10000 ;
3567: }

3569: return 1 ;

3576: }

3578: long long nonzero = 0 , peak = 0 , sumabs = 0 , i ;
3579: for ( i = 0 ; i < nsamp ; i = i + 1 ) {
3580: long long lo = ( unsigned char ) fk_micring [ ( i * 2 ) % FK_MICRING ] ;
3581: long long hi = ( unsigned char ) fk_micring [ ( i * 2 + 1 ) % FK_MICRING ] ;

3589: }

3591: peak = a ;
3592: }

3594: }

UNIT function 4035
4035: static long long fk_frame_stat ( const char * path , long long * out ) {
4036: long long n = 0 ;
4037: unsigned char * buf = fk_frame_load ( path , & n ) ;
4038: long long dib , off , w , signed_h , h , planes , bpp , compression , row , pixels , need ;
4039: if ( buf = = 0 | | n < 54 ) {
4040: free ( buf ) ;
4041: return - 1 ;
4042: }
4043: if ( buf [ 0 ] ! = 'B' | | buf [ 1 ] ! = 'M' ) {
4044: free ( buf ) ;
4045: return - 1 ;
4046: }
4047: dib = fk_rd32 ( buf + 14 ) ;
4048: off = fk_rd32 ( buf + 10 ) ;
4049: w = fk_srd32 ( buf + 18 ) ;
4050: signed_h = fk_srd32 ( buf + 22 ) ;
4051: planes = fk_rd16 ( buf + 26 ) ;
4052: bpp = fk_rd16 ( buf + 28 ) ;
4053: compression = fk_rd32 ( buf + 30 ) ;
4054: if ( dib < 40 | | dib > n - 14 | | off < 14 + dib | | off > n | | planes ! = 1 | | bpp ! = 24 | |
4055: compression ! = 0 | | w < = 0 | | signed_h = = 0 ) {
4056: free ( buf ) ;
4057: return - 1 ;
4058: }
4059: h = signed_h < 0 ? 0 - signed_h : signed_h ;
4060: row = ( w * 3 + 3 ) & ~ 3LL ;
4061: if ( row < = 0 | | h > ( 9223372036854775807LL - off ) / row ) {
4062: free ( buf ) ;
4063: return - 1 ;
4064: }

4066: if ( need > n | | w > 9223372036854775807LL / h ) {
4067: free ( buf ) ;
4068: return - 1 ;
4069: }
4070: pixels = w * h ;
4071: if ( pixels > 9223372036854775807LL / 255 ) {
4072: free ( buf ) ;
4073: return - 1 ;
4074: }
4075: long long sum = 0 , dark = 0 , ls = 0 , cs = 0 , rs = 0 , lc = 0 , cc = 0 , rc = 0 , cnt = 0 ;
4076: long long y = 0 ;
4077: while ( y < h ) {
4078: long long x = 0 ;

4080: long long idx = off + y * row + x * 3 ;
4081: long long lum = ( ( long long ) buf [ idx ] + ( long long ) buf [ idx + 1 ] +
4082: ( long long ) buf [ idx + 2 ] ) /
4083: 3 ;
4084: sum = sum + lum ;
4085: if ( lum < 60 ) {
4086: dark = dark + 1 ;
4087: }

4089: ls = ls + lum ;
4090: lc = lc + 1 ;
4091: } else if ( x < ( 2 * w ) / 3 ) {
4092: cs = cs + lum ;
4093: cc = cc + 1 ;
4094: } else {
4095: rs = rs + lum ;
4096: rc = rc + 1 ;
4097: }
4098: cnt = cnt + 1 ;

4100: }
4101: y = y + 1 ;
4102: }
4103: long long mean = cnt ? sum / cnt : 0 ;
4104: long long darkpct = cnt ? ( dark * 100 ) / cnt : 0 ;
4105: long long lm = lc ? ls / lc : 0 ;
4106: long long cm = cc ? cs / cc : 0 ;
4107: long long rm = rc ? rs / rc : 0 ;
4108: long long side = ( lm < = cm & & lm < = rm ) ? 0 : ( ( cm < = lm & & cm < = rm ) ? 1 : 2 ) ;
4109: long long spread = ( lm > rm ? lm - rm : rm - lm ) ;
4110: long long present = ( darkpct > = 8 & & darkpct < = 75 & & spread > = 12 ) ? 1 : 0 ;
4111: out [ 0 ] = present ;
4112: out [ 1 ] = side ;
4113: out [ 2 ] = mean ;
4114: out [ 3 ] = darkpct ;
4115: out [ 4 ] = lm ;
4116: out [ 5 ] = cm ;
4117: out [ 6 ] = rm ;
4118: out [ 7 ] = w ;
4119: out [ 8 ] = h ;

4121: return 0 ;
4122: }

UNIT function 5774
5774: static void fk_inv_walk ( const char * root , const char * dir , const char * suf , long long skipv ) {
5775: DIR * d = opendir ( dir ) ;
5776: if ( ! d ) {
5777: return ;
5778: }
5779: struct dirent * e ;
5780: char path [ FK_PATH_CAP ] ;
5781: while ( ( e = readdir ( d ) ) ! = 0 ) {
5782: if ( e - > d_name [ 0 ] = = FK_CH_DOT & &
5783: ( e - > d_name [ 1 ] = = 0 | | ( e - > d_name [ 1 ] = = FK_CH_DOT & & e - > d_name [ 2 ] = = 0 ) ) ) {
5784: continue ;
5785: }
5786: if ( fk_skip_entry ( skipv , e - > d_name ) ) {
5787: continue ;

5790: if ( fk_path_is_dir ( path ) ) {
5791: fk_inv_walk ( root , path , suf , skipv ) ;
5792: } else {
5793: if ( suf [ 0 ] ! = 0 & & ! fk_suffix_match ( e - > d_name , suf ) ) {
5794: continue ;
5795: }
5796: long long rn = 0 ;

UNIT directive 5992
5992: #define FK_SOCK_CAP_INIT 1024 /* fk_sock_raw/kind: live socket handles (listeners kind 1, streams kind 2). Birth size only -- the table doubles on demand (fk_sock_grow, kernel_stat 31/32 = live cap / doublings). The old fixed 1024 closed the 1025th socket and handed back -1 with no word; only the allocator can refuse now. */

5999: fk_sock_raw = ( fk_os_socket_t * ) realloc ( fk_sock_raw , ( unsigned long ) nc * sizeof ( fk_os_socket_t ) ) ;

6001: if ( fk_sock_raw = = 0 | | fk_sock_kind = = 0 ) {
6002: fk_die ( "fk_sock_grow: out of memory growing the socket handle table" ) ;
6003: }

6005: fk_sock_kind [ i ] = 0 ;

6012: }

8042: if ( x < = 0 . 0 ) {
8043: return 0 . 0 ;

8052: x = x * 2 . 0 ;

UNIT function 11530
11530: static long long fk_gift_give ( long long gh , const char * bytes , long long n ) {
11531: if ( ! fk_gift_live ( gh ) ) {
11532: return fk_nothing ;
11533: }

11545: if ( ( s0 & 1 ) = = 0 ) {
11546: long long want = s0 ;
11547: if ( __atomic_compare_exchange_n ( gseq , & want , s0 + 1 , 0 , __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL , __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE ) ) {
11548: break ;
11549: }

11552: continue ;
11553: }

11555: spins = spins + 1 ;
11556: if ( spins > FK_GIFT_GIVE_SPINS ) {

11559: stuck = - 1 ;
11560: spins = 0 ;
11561: }
11562: }

UNIT function 12245
12245: static double fk_pow_d ( double x , double y ) {
12246: if ( y = = 0 . 0 | | x = = 1 . 0 ) { return 1 . 0 ; }
12247: if ( x ! = x | | y ! = y ) { return 0 . 0 / 0 . 0 ; }
12248: if ( x = = 0 . 0 ) { return ( y < 0 . 0 ) ? ( 1 . 0 / 0 . 0 ) : 0 . 0 ; }

12250: double yi = ( double ) ( long long ) ay ;

UNIT directive 15453
15453: #define FK_SOURCE_TEXT_CAP_INIT 8388608 /* fk_srctext: the parsed program's own source text (distinct from fk_src, the staged input buffer). Birth size only -- the buffer DOUBLES on demand (fk_srctext_reserve), so program text is not a wall. History: 262144->8388608 (2026-07-02) after a 267KB generated program was SILENTLY truncated at the old cap and the permissive reader auto-closed the amputated program -- the "N=100 cliff"; then a loud refusal at 8MB; growth since 2026-09-02 (kernel_stat 25/26 = live cap / doublings). */

UNIT declaration 15454

UNIT function 15455
15455: static void fk_srctext_reserve ( long long need ) {
15456: long long nc ;
15457: char * q ;
15458: if ( fk_srctext_cap = = 0 ) {
15460: fk_srctext = ( char * ) fk_prog_take ( 'S' , FK_PROG_SRC_BYTES ) ;
15461: fk_prog_src_shared = fk_srctext ! = 0 ;
15462: if ( fk_srctext = = 0 ) { fk_srctext = malloc ( FK_SOURCE_TEXT_CAP_INIT ) ; }
15463: if ( fk_srctext = = 0 ) {
15464: fk_die ( "fk_srctext_reserve: out of memory for the source-text buffer" ) ;
15465: }
15466: fk_srctext_cap = FK_SOURCE_TEXT_CAP_INIT ;
15467: }
15468: if ( need < = fk_srctext_cap ) {
15469: return ;
15470: }
15471: nc = fk_srctext_cap ;
15472: while ( nc < need ) {
15473: nc = nc * 2 ;
15474: }
15475: if ( fk_prog_src_shared & & nc < = FK_PROG_SRC_BYTES ) {
15476: fk_srctext_cap = nc ;
15477: fk_srctext_grows = fk_srctext_grows + 1 ;
15478: return ;
15479: }

15482: { char nm [ 32 ] ; fk_store_name ( 'S' , ( long long ) getpid ( ) , nm ) ; shm_unlink ( nm ) ; }
15483: fk_prog_src_shared = 0 ;
15484: }
15485: q = realloc ( fk_srctext , ( unsigned long ) nc ) ;
15486: if ( q = = 0 ) {
15487: fk_die ( "fk_srctext_reserve: out of memory growing the source-text buffer" ) ;
15488: }
15489: fk_srctext = q ;

UNIT function 15864

15874: sp = sp + 1 ;
15875: k = k + 2 ;
15876: } else if ( opc = = 1 ) {
15877: st [ sp ] = fk_smklit ( p [ k + 1 ] ) ;
15878: sp = sp + 1 ;

15885: c3 = st [ sp - 1 ] ;
15886: sp = sp - 1 ;
15887: }

15890: sp = sp - 1 ;
15891: }

15894: sp = sp - 1 ;

15897: sp = sp + 1 ;

UNIT function 17290
17290: static void fk_sskip_at ( long long * pp ) {
17291: long long p = * pp ;
17292: while ( p < fk_slen ) {
17293: char c = fk_srctext [ p ] ;
17294: if ( fk_sws ( c ) ) {
17295: p = p + 1 ;
17296: } else if ( c = = FK_CH_SEMI ) {
17297: while ( p < fk_slen & & fk_srctext [ p ] ! = FK_CH_LF ) {
17298: p = p + 1 ;
17299: }
17300: } else {
17301: break ;
17302: }
17303: }
17304: * pp = p ;
17305: }

UNIT function 17306
17306: static long long fk_skip_balanced ( long long p ) {
17309: fk_sskip_at ( & p ) ;
17310: if ( p > = fk_slen ) {
17311: return p ;
17312: }
17313: if ( fk_srctext [ p ] = = FK_CH_LPAREN ) {
17314: long long depth = 1 ;
17315: p = p + 1 ;
17316: while ( p < fk_slen & & depth > 0 ) {
17317: char c = fk_srctext [ p ] ;
17318: if ( c = = FK_CH_SEMI ) {
17319: while ( p < fk_slen & & fk_srctext [ p ] ! = FK_CH_LF ) {
17320: p = p + 1 ;
17321: }
17322: continue ;
17323: }
17330: if ( c = = FK_CH_DQUOTE ) {
17331: p = p + 1 ;
17332: while ( p < fk_slen & & fk_srctext [ p ] ! = FK_CH_DQUOTE ) {
17333: if ( fk_srctext [ p ] = = FK_CH_BACKSLASH & & p + 1 < fk_slen ) {
17334: p = p + 1 ;
17335: }
17336: p = p + 1 ;
17337: }
17338: if ( p < fk_slen ) {
17339: p = p + 1 ;
17340: }
17341: continue ;
17342: }
17343: if ( c = = FK_CH_LPAREN ) {
17344: depth = depth + 1 ;
17345: } else if ( c = = FK_CH_RPAREN ) {
17346: depth = depth - 1 ;
17347: }
17348: p = p + 1 ;
17349: }
17350: return p ;
17351: }
17352: return fk_sym_end ( p ) ;
17353: }

UNIT function 17355
17355: static void fk_prescan_form ( long long * pp ) {
17356: long long p = * pp ;
17357: fk_sskip_at ( & p ) ;
17358: if ( p > = fk_slen | | fk_srctext [ p ] ! = FK_CH_LPAREN ) {
17359: * pp = fk_skip_balanced ( p ) ;
17360: return ;
17361: }
17362: long long h = p + 1 ;
17363: while ( h < fk_slen & & fk_sws ( fk_srctext [ h ] ) ) {
17364: h = h + 1 ;
17365: }
17366: long long he = fk_sym_end ( h ) ;
17367: if ( fk_sym_eq ( h , he - h , "do" ) ) {
17369: long long q = he ;
17370: fk_prescan_seq ( & q ) ;
17371: * pp = q ;
17372: return ;
17373: }
17374: if ( fk_sym_eq ( h , he - h , "defn" ) ) {
17375: long long ns = he ;
17376: fk_sskip_at ( & ns ) ;
17377: long long ne = fk_sym_end ( ns ) ;
17378: long long nlen = ne - ns ;
17381: long long idx = fk_defn_next ;
17382: fk_defn_next = fk_defn_next + 1 ;
17383: fk_fn_reserve ( fk_defn_next ) ;
17384: fk_fnsym_s [ fk_fntop ] = ns ;
17385: fk_fnsym_n [ fk_fntop ] = nlen ;
17386: fk_fnidx [ fk_fntop ] = idx ;
17387: fk_live_note_defn ( fk_fntop ) ;
17388: fk_fntop = fk_fntop + 1 ;
17391: long long a = ne ;
17392: fk_sskip_at ( & a ) ;
17393: long long na = 0 ;
17394: if ( a < fk_slen & & fk_srctext [ a ] = = FK_CH_LPAREN ) {
17395: a = a + 1 ;
17396: while ( 1 ) {
17397: fk_sskip_at ( & a ) ;
17398: if ( a > = fk_slen | | fk_srctext [ a ] = = FK_CH_RPAREN ) {
17399: break ;
17400: }
17401: a = fk_sym_end ( a ) ;
17402: na = na + 1 ;
17403: }
17404: }
17405: fk_fnar [ idx ] = na ;
17406: * pp = fk_skip_balanced ( p ) ;
17408: return ;
17409: }
17410: * pp = fk_skip_balanced ( p ) ;
17411: }

UNIT function 17412

17417: * pp = p ;
17418: return ;

17421: * pp = p + 1 ;
17422: return ;
17423: }
17424: fk_prescan_form ( & p ) ;
17425: }
17426: }

17722: }

18108: if ( dir_n + token_n + 1 > cap ) {
18109: return 0 ;
18110: }
18111: i = 0 ;
18112: while ( i < dir_n ) {
18113: out [ i ] = owner_path [ i ] ;
18114: i = i + 1 ;
18115: }
18116: j = 0 ;
18117: while ( j < token_n ) {
18118: out [ i + j ] = token [ j ] ;
18119: j = j + 1 ;
18120: }
18121: out [ i + j ] = 0 ;
18122: return 1 ;
18123: }

UNIT function 18156
18156: static int fk_src_unit_hash_range ( long long start , long long end , char * out , long long cap ) {
18157: long long pos = 0 ;
18158: long long i = start ;
18159: char canon [ FK_PATH_CAP ] ;
18160: unsigned long long fold = 14695981039346656037ULL ;
18161: long long count = 0 ;
18171: while ( i < end & & i < fk_src_dep_count ) {
18172: const char * cid = fk_path_canon_id ( fk_src_dep_path [ i ] , canon ) ;
18173: fold = fold ^ fk_bytes_fnv1a ( cid , fk_path_len ( cid ) ) ;
18174: fold = fold * 1099511628211ULL ;
18175: fold = fold ^ ( unsigned long long ) fk_src_dep_mtime [ i ] ;
18176: fold = fold * 1099511628211ULL ;
18177: fold = fold ^ ( unsigned long long ) fk_src_dep_size [ i ] ;
18178: fold = fold * 1099511628211ULL ;
18179: fold = fold ^ fk_src_dep_digest [ i ] ;
18180: fold = fold * 1099511628211ULL ;
18181: count = count + 1 ;
18182: i = i + 1 ;
18183: }
18184: if ( ! fk_source_hash_append ( out , cap , & pos , "fk-unit-v3|n=" ) | |
18185: ! fk_source_hash_append_ll ( out , cap , & pos , count ) | |
18186: ! fk_source_hash_append ( out , cap , & pos , "|" ) | |
18187: ! fk_source_hash_append_ll ( out , cap , & pos , ( long long ) ( fold > > 1 ) ) ) {
18188: return 0 ;
18189: }
18190: return 1 ;
18191: }

UNIT function 18378
18378: static int fk_src_collect_preludes ( const char * owner_path , const char * text , long long n ,
18379: long long owner_idx ) {

18385: long long line_end = i ;

18388: }

18393: comment = scan + 1 ;
18394: break ;
18395: }
18396: scan = scan + 1 ;
18397: }

18401: scan = scan + 1 ;
18402: }

18404: return 0 ;

18410: j = j + 1 ;

18419: text [ p ] = = FK_CH_COMMA ) ) {
18420: p = p + 1 ;
18421: }

18423: break ;

18427: text [ p ] ! = FK_CH_COMMA & & text [ p ] ! = FK_CH_LF & &
18428: text [ p ] ! = FK_CH_CR ) {
18429: p = p + 1 ;
18430: }

18436: p = p + 1 ;

18442: p = p + 1 ;

18445: p = p + 1 ;

18448: p = p + 1 ;

18451: p = p + 1 ;
18452: }
18453: continue ;
18454: }

18510: }

UNIT function 18532
18532: static int fk_src_collect_file ( const char * path , long long parent_idx ) {
18533: if ( fk_src_dep_index ( path ) > = 0 ) {
18534: return 1 ;
18535: }
18536: long long mtime = fk_path_mtime_raw ( path ) ;
18537: long long size = fk_path_size_raw ( path ) ;
18538: if ( mtime < = 0 | | size < 0 ) {
18539: fk_diag_path ( "error" , path , "dependency source is missing or not stat-readable" ) ;
18540: return 0 ;
18541: }
18542: #if defined(_WIN32)
 int fd = open ( path , 0x8000 ) ;
18544: #else
 int fd = open ( path , 0 ) ;
18546: #endif
 if ( fd < 0 ) {
18548: fk_diag_path ( "error" , path , "dependency source could not be opened" ) ;
18549: return 0 ;
18550: }
18551: long long got = fk_read_all_dynamic ( fd , size + 1 ) ;
18552: close ( fd ) ;
18553: if ( got < 0 ) {
18554: if ( got = = - 3 ) {
18555: fk_diag_path ( "error" , path ,
18556: "dependency source could not grow the dynamic read buffer" ) ;
18557: } else {
18558: fk_diag_path ( "error" , path , "dependency source could not be read" ) ;
18559: }
18560: return 0 ;
18561: }
18562: char * owned = malloc ( ( unsigned long ) got + 1 ) ;
18563: if ( owned = = 0 ) {
18564: fk_die ( "fk_run_src: out of memory reading dependency source" ) ;
18565: }
18566: long long i = 0 ;
18567: while ( i < got ) {
18568: owned [ i ] = fk_buf [ i ] ;
18569: i = i + 1 ;
18570: }
18571: owned [ got ] = 0 ;
18572: return fk_src_collect_bytes ( path , owned , got , mtime , size , parent_idx ) ;
18573: }

UNIT function 18578
18578: static int fk_src_collect_bytes ( const char * path , char * owned , long long got ,
18579: long long mtime , long long size , long long parent_idx ) {
18580: long long i = 0 ;
18581: ( void ) i ;
18582: if ( ! fk_src_dep_reserve ( fk_src_dep_count + 1 ) ) {
18583: free ( owned ) ;
18584: fk_diag_path ( "error" , path , "could not grow .fk dependency metadata" ) ;
18585: return 0 ;
18586: }
18587: long long idx = fk_src_dep_count ;
18588: fk_cstr_copy ( fk_src_dep_path [ fk_src_dep_count ] , path , FK_PATH_CAP ) ;
18589: fk_src_dep_mtime [ fk_src_dep_count ] = mtime ;
18590: fk_src_dep_size [ fk_src_dep_count ] = size ;
18591: fk_src_dep_digest [ fk_src_dep_count ] = fk_bytes_fnv1a ( owned , got ) ;
18592: fk_src_dep_parent [ fk_src_dep_count ] = parent_idx ;
18593: fk_src_dep_end [ fk_src_dep_count ] = fk_src_dep_count + 1 ;
18594: fk_src_dep_text_off [ fk_src_dep_count ] = 0 ;
18595: fk_src_dep_text_len [ fk_src_dep_count ] = 0 ;
18596: fk_src_dep_lowered [ fk_src_dep_count ] = 0 ;
18597: fk_src_dep_count = fk_src_dep_count + 1 ;
18598: if ( fk_cstr_eq ( path , fk_src_root_path ) ) {
18599: fk_src_root_reserve ( got + 1 ) ;
18600: i = 0 ;
18601: while ( i < got ) {
18602: fk_src_root_text [ i ] = owned [ i ] ;
18603: i = i + 1 ;
18604: }
18605: fk_src_root_text [ got ] = 0 ;
18606: fk_src_root_len = got ;
18607: }
18608: if ( ! fk_src_collect_preludes ( path , owned , got , idx ) ) {
18609: free ( owned ) ;
18610: return 0 ;
18611: }

19145: if ( rename ( fkb_tmp , fkb_path ) ! = 0 ) {
19146: unlink ( fkb_tmp ) ;
19147: return 0 ;
19148: }
19149: return 1 ;
19150: }

UNIT function 19173

19175: fk_fkb_mark_bad ( "truncated artifact" ) ;
19176: return 0 ;
19177: }
19178: return ( long long ) ( unsigned char ) fk_buf [ fk_fkb_pos + + ] ;
19179: }

UNIT function 19180
19180: static long long fk_fkb_read_u32 ( void ) {
19181: long long a = fk_fkb_read_u8 ( ) ;
19182: long long b = fk_fkb_read_u8 ( ) ;
19183: long long c = fk_fkb_read_u8 ( ) ;
19184: long long d = fk_fkb_read_u8 ( ) ;
19185: return ( a < < 24 ) | ( b < < 16 ) | ( c < < 8 ) | d ;
19186: }

UNIT function 19187
19187: static long long fk_fkb_read_signed ( void ) {
19188: long long sign = fk_fkb_read_u8 ( ) ;
19189: long long hi = fk_fkb_read_u32 ( ) ;
19190: long long lo = fk_fkb_read_u32 ( ) ;
19191: if ( hi > 2147483647LL ) {
19193: fk_fkb_mark_bad ( "signed magnitude exceeds 63 bits" ) ;
19194: return 0 ;
19195: }
19196: long long mag = ( hi < < 32 ) | lo ;
19197: if ( sign = = 0 ) {
19198: return mag ;
19199: }
19200: if ( sign = = 1 ) {
19201: return - mag ;
19202: }
19203: fk_fkb_mark_bad ( "malformed signed integer" ) ;
19204: return 0 ;
19205: }

19209: fk_fkb_mark_bad ( "truncated string" ) ;
19210: return ;

19213: }

19223: while ( i < n ) {

19225: ok = 0 ;

UNIT function 19242
19242: static void fk_fkb_read_table_string ( void ) {
19243: long long n = fk_fkb_read_u32 ( ) ;
19244: if ( n < 0 | | fk_fkb_pos + n > fk_fkb_len ) {
19245: fk_fkb_mark_bad ( "truncated table string" ) ;
19246: return ;
19247: }
19248: if ( fk_sp > = fk_scap_s ) {
19249: fk_so = ( long long * ) fk_store_grow ( 'O' , fk_so , fk_scap_s * 8 , fk_scap_s * 16 , FK_STORE_STR_CELLS * 8 , 0 ) ;
19250: fk_sl = ( long long * ) fk_store_grow ( 'L' , fk_sl , fk_scap_s * 8 , fk_scap_s * 16 , FK_STORE_STR_CELLS * 8 , 0 ) ;
19251: fk_scap_s = fk_scap_s * 2 ;

19255: { long long z = fk_scap_s / 2 ; while ( z < fk_scap_s ) { fk_sdead [ z ] = 0 ; z = z + 1 ; } }

19258: }
19259: }
19260: while ( fk_sbp + n > fk_scap_b ) {
19261: fk_sb = ( char * ) fk_store_grow ( 's' , fk_sb , fk_scap_b , fk_scap_b * 2 , FK_STORE_STR_BYTES , 0 ) ;
19262: fk_scap_b = fk_scap_b * 2 ;
19263: fk_sb_check ( ) ;
19264: }
19265: long long start = fk_sbp ;
19266: long long j = 0 ;
19267: while ( j < n ) {
19268: fk_sb [ fk_sbp + + ] = fk_buf [ fk_fkb_pos + + ] ;
19269: j = j + 1 ;
19270: }

19273: long long bucket = fk_str_hash ( start , n ) ;
19274: fk_snext [ fk_sp ] = fk_shash [ bucket ] ;
19275: fk_shash [ bucket ] = fk_sp ;
19276: fk_sp = fk_sp + 1 ;
19277: }

UNIT function 19284
19284: static long long fk_fkb_node_arity_for_tag ( long long tag ) {

19286: tag = = 243 ) {
19287: return 0 ;
19288: }

19294: return 3 ;
19295: }
19296: if ( tag = = 7 | | tag = = 14 | | tag = = 45 | | tag = = 72 | | tag = = 74 | | tag = = 75 | |
19297: tag = = 76 | | tag = = 78 | | tag = = 110 | | tag = = 138 ) {
19298: return 1 ;

19304: }

19306: return - 2 ;
19307: }

19309: return 1 ;
19310: }

19314: return 1 ;
19315: }

19323: return 3 ;
19324: }

19328: }

UNIT function 19331
19331: static long long fk_fkb_remap_field ( long long tag , long long field , long long value ,
19332: long long node_base , long long fn_base ,
19333: long long str_base ) {

19335: return value ;
19336: }
19337: if ( ( tag = = 24 | | tag = = 50 ) & & field = = 1 ) {
19338: return value + str_base ;
19339: }
19340: if ( ( tag = = 12 | | tag = = 240 | | tag = = 241 | | tag = = 243 ) & & field = = 1 ) {
19341: return fk_fkb_remap_fn ( value , fn_base ) ;

19344: return value + node_base ;
19345: }
19346: if ( tag = = 243 & & field = = 2 ) {
19349: return value + node_base ;
19350: }

19352: return value + node_base ;
19353: }

19358: return 0 ;

19362: }

UNIT function 19369
19369: static int fk_fkb_read_symbol_to_srctext ( long long * start , long long * len ) {

19385: fk_slen = fk_slen + 1 ;

19391: fk_srctext [ fk_slen ] = 0 ;

19393: }

UNIT function 19634
19634: static int fk_src_load_fkb_checked ( const char * fkb_path , const char * expected_src_path ,
19635: const char * expected_source_hash ,
19636: long long expected_source_mtime ) {
19637: #if defined(_WIN32)

19641: #endif

19643: return 0 ;
19644: }

19646: close ( fd ) ;

19648: fk_fkb_begin ( 0 ) ;

UNIT function 19981
19981: static void fk_src_check_balance ( void ) {
19982: long long p = 0 ;
19983: long long depth = 0 ;
19984: long long outermost_open = - 1 ;
19985: while ( p < fk_slen ) {
19986: char c = fk_srctext [ p ] ;
19987: if ( c = = FK_CH_SEMI ) {
19988: while ( p < fk_slen & & fk_srctext [ p ] ! = FK_CH_LF ) {
19989: p = p + 1 ;
19990: }
19991: continue ;
19992: }
19993: if ( c = = FK_CH_DQUOTE ) {
19994: p = p + 1 ;
19995: while ( p < fk_slen & & fk_srctext [ p ] ! = FK_CH_DQUOTE ) {
19996: if ( fk_srctext [ p ] = = FK_CH_BACKSLASH & & p + 1 < fk_slen ) {
19997: p = p + 1 ;
19998: }
19999: p = p + 1 ;
20000: }
20001: if ( p < fk_slen ) {
20002: p = p + 1 ;
20003: }
20004: continue ;
20005: }
20006: if ( c = = FK_CH_LPAREN ) {
20007: if ( depth = = 0 ) {
20008: outermost_open = p ;
20009: }
20010: depth = depth + 1 ;
20011: } else if ( c = = FK_CH_RPAREN ) {
20012: depth = depth - 1 ;
20013: if ( depth < 0 ) {
20014: fk_diag ( FK_DIAG_ERR , p ,
20015: "[unbalanced-source] stray ')' closes a form that was never opened -- "
20016: "the text cannot be read as the program it claims to be, so there is "
20017: "nothing for a verdict to be of. Refusing to run" ) ;
20018: fk_src_unrunnable = 1 ;
20019: return ;
20020: }
20021: }
20022: p = p + 1 ;
20023: }
20024: if ( depth > 0 ) {
20038: fk_diag ( FK_DIAG_ERR , outermost_open > = 0 ? outermost_open : 0 ,
20039: "[input-ended-mid-form] the input ended before this form closed -- %lld open "
20040: "paren(s) remain. A stream that STOPPED and a stream that FINISHED end with the "
20041: "same terminator, so the prefix would otherwise be read as a whole program: the "
20042: "permissive reader auto-closes it and computes the right answer to the wrong "
20043: "text. Completion is not the absence of more bytes. Refusing to run" ,
20044: depth ) ;
20045: fk_src_truncated = 1 ;
20046: fk_src_unrunnable = 1 ;
20047: }
20048: }

UNIT function 20049
20049: static void fk_src_compile_current_unit ( const char * path , const char * fkb_path ,
20050: const char * sym_path , long long unit_mtime ,
20051: const char * source_hash ) {
20052: fk_spos = 0 ;
20053: fk_srctext [ fk_slen ] = 0 ;
20054: fk_src_check_balance ( ) ;

20061: fk_fn [ 0 ] = fk_smklit ( 0 ) ;
20062: fk_fn_count = 1 ;
20063: return ;
20064: }
20066: fk_prescan_defns ( ) ;

20071: fk_sskip ( ) ;

20073: break ;
20074: }

20077: if ( fk_root > = 0 ) {
20078: fk_fn [ 0 ] = fk_root ;
20079: } else if ( fk_defn_next > 1 ) {
20080: fk_fn [ 0 ] = fk_fn [ fk_defn_next - 1 ] ;
20081: }
20083: else {
20084: fk_fn [ 0 ] = fk_smklit ( 0 ) ;
20085: }
20086: fk_fn_count = fk_defn_next ;
20087: if ( fk_maxslot > 0 ) {

20093: "program image is outside the .fkb v4 signed lane (magnitude 2^63, "

20098: }

20840: return 0 ;

20844: close ( fd ) ;

UNIT function 20903
